(function(){
const T = THREE;
const PA = {};
const _v1=new T.Vector3(),_v2=new T.Vector3(),_v3=new T.Vector3(),_q1=new T.Quaternion(),_q2=new T.Quaternion(),_q3=new T.Quaternion(),_m=new T.Matrix4();
PA.make = function(root){
  const S = {root, b:{}};
  root.traverse(o=>{ if(o.isBone||o.type==='Bone'||/^(Hips|Sword)$/.test(o.name)) if(!S.b[o.name]) S.b[o.name]=o; });
  S.rq = new T.Quaternion();
  S.upd = ()=>{ root.updateMatrixWorld(true); root.getWorldQuaternion(S.rq); S.rqi = S.rq.clone().invert(); };
  S.lp = (o,out)=>{ out=out||new T.Vector3(); o.getWorldPosition(out); return root.worldToLocal(out); };
  S.lq = (o,out)=>{ out=out||new T.Quaternion(); o.getWorldQuaternion(out); return out.premultiply(S.rqi); };
  S.setLq = (o,q,w)=>{ const pq=S.lq(o.parent,new T.Quaternion()).invert(); const t=pq.multiply(q); if(w===undefined||w>=1) o.quaternion.copy(t); else o.quaternion.slerp(t,w); o.updateMatrixWorld(true); };
  S.rotW = (o,axis,ang)=>{ const q=S.lq(o,new T.Quaternion()); q.premultiply(new T.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(axis,ang)); S.setLq(o,q); };   // rotate bone about a root-space axis through its pivot
  S.aim = (bone,child,tgt,w)=>{ const a=S.lp(bone),c=S.lp(child); const d0=c.sub(a); if(d0.lengthSq()<1e-10) return; d0.normalize(); const d1=tgt.clone().sub(a); if(d1.lengthSq()<1e-10) return; d1.normalize();
     const q=new T.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(d0,d1).multiply(S.lq(bone)); S.setLq(bone,q,w); };
  S.len = (a,b)=>S.lp(a).distanceTo(S.lp(b));
  // two-bone IK: put end's pivot at tgt, middle joint bent toward pole (root-space point)
  S.ik = (up,mid,end,tgt,pole,w)=>{ const A=S.lp(up), l1=S.len(up,mid), l2=S.len(mid,end); let d=tgt.clone().sub(A); let L=d.length(); const mx=(l1+l2)*0.9995; if(L>mx){ L=mx; } if(L<Math.abs(l1-l2)+1e-4) L=Math.abs(l1-l2)+1e-4; const u=d.normalize();
     const a=(l1*l1-l2*l2+L*L)/(2*L), h=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,l1*l1-a*a)); const pv=pole.clone().sub(A); pv.addScaledVector(u,-pv.dot(u)); if(pv.lengthSq()<1e-10) pv.set(0,0,1); pv.normalize();
     const J=A.clone().addScaledVector(u,a).addScaledVector(pv,h); const E=A.clone().addScaledVector(u,L);
     S.aim(up,mid,J,w); S.aim(mid,end,E,w); return J; };
  // orient a bone so its local axis ax (bone-local vector) points along root-space dir, with second local axis ax2 toward dir2 (optional)
  S.orient = (o,ax,dir,ax2,dir2,w)=>{ const q=S.lq(o); const cur=ax.clone().applyQuaternion(q).normalize(); const dq=new T.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(cur,dir.clone().normalize()); q.premultiply(dq);
     if(ax2){ const c2=ax2.clone().applyQuaternion(q); const n=dir.clone().normalize(); c2.addScaledVector(n,-c2.dot(n)).normalize(); const t2=dir2.clone(); t2.addScaledVector(n,-t2.dot(n)); if(t2.lengthSq()>1e-8){ t2.normalize(); const ang=Math.atan2(n.dot(new T.Vector3().crossVectors(c2,t2)), c2.dot(t2)); q.premultiply(new T.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(n,ang)); } }
     S.setLq(o,q,w); };
  S.axis = (o,ax)=>ax.clone().applyQuaternion(S.lq(o)).normalize();   // a bone-local axis in root space
  S.snap = (names)=>{ const r={}; for(const n of names){ const o=S.b[n]; if(o) r[n]=[o.quaternion.clone(),o.position.clone()]; } return r; };
  S.apply = (s)=>{ for(const n in s){ const o=S.b[n]; if(o){ o.quaternion.copy(s[n][0]); o.position.copy(s[n][1]); } } root.updateMatrixWorld(true); };
  S.upd(); return S;
};
// sample a clip into a pose snapshot (bones by name) using a temp mixer
PA.sample = function(root, clip, t){ const mx=new T.AnimationMixer(root); const a=mx.clipAction(clip); a.play(); mx.setTime(t); root.updateMatrixWorld(true); const r={}; root.traverse(o=>{ if(o.name) r[o.name]=[o.quaternion.clone(),o.position.clone()]; }); a.stop(); mx.uncacheRoot(root); return r; };
// bake: fn(S,t) sets pose; records quats of names (+Hips position) at fps; base snapshot applied first each frame
PA.bake = function(S, name, dur, fps, names, base, fn, opt){ opt=opt||{}; const n=Math.max(2,Math.round(dur*fps)+1), times=new Float32Array(n), Q={}, P=new Float32Array(n*3);
  for(const nm of names) Q[nm]=new Float32Array(n*4); const PP={}; if(opt.posNames) for(const nm of opt.posNames) PP[nm]=new Float32Array(n*3);
  const keep=S.snap(Object.keys(S.b));
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ const t=dur*i/(n-1); times[i]=t; S.apply(base); S.upd(); fn(S,t); S.root.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    for(const nm of names){ const o=S.b[nm]; if(!o) continue; o.quaternion.toArray(Q[nm],i*4); } S.b.Hips.position.toArray(P,i*3); if(opt.posNames) for(const nm of opt.posNames){ const o=S.b[nm]; if(o) o.position.toArray(PP[nm],i*3); } }
  S.apply(keep);
  const tr=[]; for(const nm of names){ if(!S.b[nm]) continue; const arr=Q[nm]; for(let i=1;i<n;i++){ const d=arr[i*4]*arr[i*4-4]+arr[i*4+1]*arr[i*4-3]+arr[i*4+2]*arr[i*4-2]+arr[i*4+3]*arr[i*4-1]; if(d<0) for(let k=0;k<4;k++) arr[i*4+k]*=-1; }
    tr.push(new T.QuaternionKeyframeTrack(nm+'.quaternion', times, arr)); }
  if(!opt.noHipPos) tr.push(new T.VectorKeyframeTrack('Hips.position', times, P));
  if(opt.posNames) for(const nm of opt.posNames){ if(PP[nm]) tr.push(new T.VectorKeyframeTrack(nm+'.position', times, PP[nm])); }
  if(opt.extra) for(const x of opt.extra) tr.push(x);
  return new T.AnimationClip(name, dur, tr);
};
window.PA = PA;
})();

(function(){
const T = THREE;
const PA = window.PA; const P22 = {};
const V=(x,y,z)=>new T.Vector3(x,y,z), UPV=V(0,1,0), FW=V(0,0,1), LT=V(1,0,0);
const BODY=['Hips','Spine02','Spine01','Spine','neck','Head','LeftShoulder','LeftArm','LeftForeArm','LeftHand','RightShoulder','RightArm','RightForeArm','RightHand','LeftUpLeg','LeftLeg','LeftFoot','LeftToeBase','RightUpLeg','RightLeg','RightFoot','RightToeBase'];
P22.BODY=BODY;
const rad=d=>d*Math.PI/180, sm=x=>{x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));return x*x*(3-2*x);}, lerp=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t;
P22.sm=sm;
// upright torso chain: orient bones' Y to dir (pitch fwd +, roll, yaw), Z forward
function torso(S, o){ // o: {hipY, hipX, hipZ, hipPitch, hipYaw, hipRoll, sp:[pitch,yaw,roll] x3, neck:[..], head:[..]}
  const H=S.b.Hips; H.position.y = H.position.y; 
  const setB=(b,p,y,r)=>{ const q=new T.Quaternion().setFromEuler(new T.Euler(rad(p),rad(y),rad(r),'YXZ')); const yA=UPV.clone().applyQuaternion(q), zA=FW.clone().applyQuaternion(q); S.orient(b,V(0,1,0),yA,V(0,0,1),zA); };
  setB(H,o.hp||0,o.hy||0,o.hr||0);
  const sp=o.sp||[[0,0,0],[0,0,0],[0,0,0]]; setB(S.b.Spine02,...sp[0]); setB(S.b.Spine01,...sp[1]); setB(S.b.Spine,...sp[2]);
  setB(S.b.neck,...(o.nk||[0,0,0])); setB(S.b.Head,...(o.hd||[0,0,0]));
}
P22.torso=torso;
// set hips position in model space (meters) — Hips parent is Armature (scale .01)
function hipPos(S, x,y,z){ const p=V(x,y,z); S.root.localToWorld(p); S.b.Hips.parent.worldToLocal(p); S.b.Hips.position.copy(p); S.root.updateMatrixWorld(true); }
P22.hipPos=hipPos;
// planted foot: ankle at (x,y,z), toe yaw (deg, + = outward), knee pole outward/forward
function leg(S, sd, ank, yawOut, pitch, pole){ const L=sd==='L'?'Left':'Right', sg=sd==='L'?1:-1;
  const up=S.b[L+'UpLeg'], lo=S.b[L+'Leg'], ft=S.b[L+'Foot'], toe=S.b[L+'ToeBase'];
  const hp=S.lp(up); const pl=pole||hp.clone().add(V(sg*0.12,-0.45,0.9));
  S.ik(up,lo,ft,ank,pl);
  const yw=rad(yawOut||0)*sg, pc=rad(pitch===undefined?34:pitch);
  const fdir=V(Math.sin(yw)*Math.cos(pc), -Math.sin(pc), Math.cos(yw)*Math.cos(pc));
  S.orient(ft,V(0,1,0),fdir,V(1,0,0),V(Math.cos(yw),0,-Math.sin(yw)));
  const tdir=V(Math.sin(yw),0,Math.cos(yw)); S.orient(toe,V(0,1,0),tdir);
}
P22.leg=leg;
// arm: hand pivot to target, elbow pole, then hand orientation: axis ax(local) -> dir, ax2 -> dir2
function arm(S, sd, tgt, pole, hax, hdir, hax2, hdir2){ const L=sd==='L'?'Left':'Right';
  const ua=S.b[L+'Arm'], fa=S.b[L+'ForeArm'], h=S.b[L+'Hand'];
  S.ik(ua,fa,h,tgt,pole); if(hax) S.orient(h,hax,hdir,hax2,hdir2);
}
P22.arm=arm;
// sword axis in RightHand-local (pommel->tip), fist centre (m in model space via hand)
P22.SWAX = V(-0.255,0.58,0.775).normalize();
// cape helpers: cape bones are children of Spine; move a cape bone to root-space point keeping its root-space rotation
function capeBones(S){ if(S.cape) return S.cape; const c=[]; for(const n in S.b){ const m=/^Cape(\d\d)_(\d)$/.exec(n); if(m) c.push({o:S.b[n], col:+m[1], row:+m[2]}); } S.cape=c; return c; }
P22.capeBones=capeBones;
// rotate whole cape about a root-space axis through pivot by ang*f(row)
function capeSwing(S, axis, pivot, angFn){ const C=capeBones(S); const tmp=[]; for(const c of C){ const p=S.lp(c.o), q=S.lq(c.o); tmp.push([c,p,q]); }
  for(const [c,p,q] of tmp){ const a=angFn(c.row,c.col); if(Math.abs(a)<1e-5) continue; const rq=new T.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(axis,a); const np=p.clone().sub(pivot).applyQuaternion(rq).add(pivot); const nq=q.clone().premultiply(rq);
    S.root.localToWorld(np); c.o.parent.worldToLocal(np); c.o.position.copy(np); S.setLq(c.o,nq); } }
P22.capeSwing=capeSwing;
// push cape bones out of leg capsules (root space)
function segDist(p,a,b,out){ const ab=b.clone().sub(a), t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,p.clone().sub(a).dot(ab)/ab.lengthSq())); out.copy(a).addScaledVector(ab,t); return p.distanceTo(out); }
function capePush(S, extra){ const C=capeBones(S); const segs=[]; const B=S.b;
  for(const L of ['Left','Right']){ segs.push([S.lp(B[L+'UpLeg']),S.lp(B[L+'Leg']),0.095]); segs.push([S.lp(B[L+'Leg']),S.lp(B[L+'Foot']),0.07]); }
  segs.push([S.lp(B.Hips).add(V(0,-0.05,0)),S.lp(B.Spine),0.14]);
  if(extra) for(const e of extra) segs.push(e);
  const cl=V(0,0,0); let mx=0;
  for(const c of C){ if(c.row<1) continue; const p=S.lp(c.o); let moved=false;
    for(const [a,b,r] of segs){ const d=segDist(p,a,b,cl); if(d<r+0.012){ const n=p.clone().sub(cl); if(n.lengthSq()<1e-8) n.set(0,0,-1); n.normalize(); mx=Math.max(mx,r+0.012-d); p.copy(cl).addScaledVector(n,r+0.012); moved=true; } }
    if(moved){ S.root.localToWorld(p); c.o.parent.worldToLocal(p); c.o.position.copy(p); c.o.updateMatrixWorld(true); } }
  return mx; }
P22.capePush=capePush;
window.P22=P22;
})();

P22.heroIdle = function(S,t,o){ o=o||{}; const T=THREE; const V=(x,y,z)=>new T.Vector3(x,y,z);
  const w=2*Math.PI*t/4, br=Math.sin(w), sw=Math.sin(w*0.5+0.7);
  P22.hipPos(S, 0.006*sw, 0.995+0.003*br, -0.012);
  P22.torso(S,{hp:2, hr:0.8*sw, hy:0, sp:[[0,0,-0.4*sw],[-2+0.5*br,0,0],[-3+0.9*br,0,0]], nk:[6,0,0], hd:[-4-0.6*br,0,0]});
  P22.leg(S,'L',V(0.13,0.144,0.05),9,34); P22.leg(S,'R',V(-0.13,0.144,-0.05),11,34);
  const b2=0.006*br;
  P22.arm(S,'L',V(0.235,0.915+b2,0.03),V(0.7,1.0,-0.6), V(0,1,0),V(0.06,-0.97,0.22).normalize(), V(1,0,0),V(-1,0,0.15));
  if(o.noSword) P22.arm(S,'R',V(-0.235,0.915+b2,0.03),V(-0.7,1.0,-0.6), V(0,1,0),V(-0.06,-0.97,0.22).normalize(), V(1,0,0),V(-1,0,-0.15));
  else P22.arm(S,'R',V(-0.24,0.925+b2,0.075),V(-0.7,1.0,-0.6), P22.SWAX, V(-0.10,-0.90,0.42).normalize(), V(1,0,0), V(o.rx||0.0,o.ry||0.4,o.rz||-1.0));
};

P22.capeAvg = function(R, clip, N){ const T=THREE; const acc={}; N=N||16;
  for(let k=0;k<N;k++){ const s=PA.sample(R, clip, k*clip.duration/N); for(const n in s){ if(!/^Cape/.test(n)) continue; if(!acc[n]) acc[n]=[new T.Vector4(),new T.Vector3()]; const q=s[n][0]; const a=acc[n][0]; const sg=(a.x*q.x+a.y*q.y+a.z*q.z+a.w*q.w)<0?-1:1; a.x+=sg*q.x;a.y+=sg*q.y;a.z+=sg*q.z;a.w+=sg*q.w; acc[n][1].add(s[n][1]); } }
  const out={}; for(const n in acc){ const a=acc[n][0]; out[n]=[new T.Quaternion(a.x,a.y,a.z,a.w).normalize(), acc[n][1].multiplyScalar(1/N)]; } return out; };
P22.capeIdleSway = function(S,t,amp){ const T=THREE; const C=P22.capeBones(S); const piv=new T.Vector3(); let k=0; for(const c of C) if(c.row===0){ piv.add(S.lp(c.o)); k++; } piv.multiplyScalar(1/k);
  const w=2*Math.PI*t/4; P22.capeSwing(S,new T.Vector3(1,0,0),piv,(row)=>(amp||0.035)*(0.6+0.4*Math.sin(w+row*0.35))*(row/8));
  P22.capeSwing(S,new T.Vector3(0,0,1),piv,(row)=>0.02*Math.sin(w*0.5+0.7)*(row/8)); };
P22.buildIdle = function(S, R, clips){ const base=PA.sample(R, clips.Walk, 0.52); Object.assign(base, P22.capeAvg(R, clips.Walk, 16)); const cn=P22.capeBones(S).map(c=>c.o.name);
  const mk=(name,o)=>PA.bake(S, name, 4, 15, P22.BODY.concat(cn), base, (S,t)=>{ P22.heroIdle(S,t,o); P22.capeIdleSway(S,t); P22.capePush(S); }, {posNames:cn});
  return {HeroIdle: mk('HeroIdle',{}), HeroIdleFist: mk('HeroIdleFist',{noSword:true})}; };

P22.kf = function(keys, t){ let i=0; while(i<keys.length-2 && t>keys[i+1].t) i++; const a=keys[i], b=keys[i+1]; const u=b.t>a.t? P22.sm((t-a.t)/(b.t-a.t)):1;
  const out={}; for(const k in a){ if(k==='t') continue; const x=a[k], y=(b[k]===undefined?x:b[k]); out[k]= Array.isArray(x)? x.map((v,j)=>v+(y[j]-v)*u) : x+(y-x)*u; } return out; };
P22.fight = function(S,p,o){ const T=THREE; const V=(x,y,z)=>new T.Vector3(x,y,z); o=o||{};
  P22.hipPos(S, p.hp[0], p.hp[1], p.hp[2]);
  P22.torso(S,{hp:p.lean*0.3, hy:p.hy, hr:p.roll||0, sp:[[p.lean*0.3,p.ty*0.3,0],[p.lean*0.3,p.ty*0.35,0],[p.lean*0.4,p.ty*0.35,0]], nk:[6, -(p.hy+p.ty)*0.5, 0], hd:[3, -(p.hy+p.ty)*0.5+(p.hdy||0), 0]});
  if(!o.noLegs){ P22.leg(S,'L',V(0.11,0.144,0.17),18,34,V(0.5,0.6,1.2)); P22.leg(S,'R',V(-0.17,0.144+p.heel*0.05,-0.17),40+p.heel*25,34+p.heel*30,V(-0.6,0.6,1.0)); }
  const swl=V(0.255,0.58,0.775).normalize();
  const dirL=V(...p.ld).normalize(), dirR=V(...p.rd).normalize();
  P22.arm(S,'L',V(...p.lh),V(...p.le), V(0,1,0),dirL, swl,V(...p.lg));
  P22.arm(S,'R',V(...p.rh),V(...p.re), V(0,1,0),dirR, P22.SWAX,V(...p.rg));
};
P22.GUARD = {hp:[0.0,0.955,-0.02], lean:6, hy:-28, ty:-4, heel:0, lh:[0.10,1.30,0.22], le:[0.6,0.6,0.0], ld:[-0.15,0.75,0.65], lg:[-1,0.3,0], rh:[-0.06,1.27,0.12], re:[-0.6,0.5,-0.3], rd:[0.15,0.75,0.6], rg:[1,0.3,0]};
P22.punchKeys = function(side){ const G=P22.GUARD; const k=(t,o)=>Object.assign({t},G,o);
  if(side==='R') return [k(0,{}), k(0.10,{hy:-34,ty:-8,rh:[-0.08,1.27,0.06]}), k(0.27,{hy:4,ty:22,lean:10,heel:1,hp:[0.02,0.945,0.0], rh:[0.03,1.36,0.58], re:[-0.8,1.2,0.0], rd:[0.05,0.0,1], rg:[1,-0.1,0], lh:[0.13,1.31,0.17]}),
     k(0.38,{hy:2,ty:20,lean:10,heel:1,hp:[0.02,0.945,0.0], rh:[0.02,1.36,0.55], re:[-0.8,1.2,0.0], rd:[0.05,0.0,1], rg:[1,-0.1,0], lh:[0.13,1.31,0.17]}), k(0.62,{})];
  return [k(0,{}), k(0.08,{hy:-24,ty:-2}), k(0.22,{hy:-38,ty:-14,lean:9,hp:[0.0,0.95,0.01], lh:[0.04,1.37,0.60], le:[0.8,1.0,0.0], ld:[-0.05,0.0,1], lg:[-1,-0.1,0], rh:[-0.08,1.30,0.15]}),
     k(0.32,{hy:-36,ty:-12,lean:9,hp:[0.0,0.95,0.01], lh:[0.04,1.37,0.57], le:[0.8,1.0,0.0], ld:[-0.05,0.0,1], lg:[-1,-0.1,0], rh:[-0.08,1.30,0.15]}), k(0.56,{})];
};
P22.buildPunch = function(S, R, clips){ const base=PA.sample(R, clips.Walk, 0.52); Object.assign(base, P22.capeAvg(R, clips.Walk, 16)); const cn=P22.capeBones(S).map(c=>c.o.name); const out={};
  for(const sd of ['R','L']){ const K=P22.punchKeys(sd), dur=K[K.length-1].t;
    out['Punch'+sd]=PA.bake(S,'Punch'+sd,dur,30,P22.BODY.concat(cn),base,(S,t)=>{ P22.fight(S,P22.kf(K,t)); P22.capeIdleSway(S,t*3,0.05); P22.capePush(S); },{posNames:cn}); }
  return out; };

P22.makeLeftFist = function(R){ const T=THREE; let b=null, hl=null; R.traverse(o=>{ if(o.isSkinnedMesh){ if(o.material&&o.material.name==='Fist') b=o; if(o.material&&o.material.name==='HandL') hl=o; } });
  if(!b||!hl) return null; if(R.userData.lfist) return R.userData.lfist; const sk=b.skeleton, li=sk.bones.findIndex(x=>x.name==='LeftHand'), ri=sk.bones.findIndex(x=>x.name==='RightHand'); if(li<0||ri<0) return null;
  const g=b.geometry, n=g.attributes.position.count, pos=new Float32Array(n*3), nor=new Float32Array(n*3), si=new Uint16Array(n*4), sw=new Float32Array(n*4);
  const A=new T.Matrix4().multiplyMatrices(sk.boneInverses[ri], b.bindMatrix); const Sx=new T.Matrix4().makeScale(-1,1,1);
  const Bl=new T.Matrix4().copy(sk.boneInverses[li]).invert(); const M=new T.Matrix4().multiplyMatrices(new T.Matrix4().copy(b.bindMatrix).invert(), Bl).multiply(Sx).multiply(A);
  const Nm=new T.Matrix3().getNormalMatrix(M); const v=new T.Vector3();
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ v.fromBufferAttribute(g.attributes.position,i).applyMatrix4(M); pos[i*3]=v.x;pos[i*3+1]=v.y;pos[i*3+2]=v.z; v.fromBufferAttribute(g.attributes.normal,i).applyMatrix3(Nm).normalize(); nor[i*3]=v.x;nor[i*3+1]=v.y;nor[i*3+2]=v.z; si[i*4]=li; sw[i*4]=1; }
  const ng=new T.BufferGeometry(); ng.setAttribute('position',new T.BufferAttribute(pos,3)); ng.setAttribute('normal',new T.BufferAttribute(nor,3)); ng.setAttribute('uv',g.attributes.uv); ng.setAttribute('skinIndex',new T.BufferAttribute(si,4)); ng.setAttribute('skinWeight',new T.BufferAttribute(sw,4));
  const ix=g.index.array, ni=new (ix.constructor)(ix.length); for(let i=0;i<ix.length;i+=3){ ni[i]=ix[i]; ni[i+1]=ix[i+2]; ni[i+2]=ix[i+1]; } ng.setIndex(new T.BufferAttribute(ni,1)); ng.computeBoundingSphere();
  const m=new T.SkinnedMesh(ng, b.material); m.name='PlayerFistL'; m.castShadow=b.castShadow; m.receiveShadow=b.receiveShadow; m.frustumCulled=b.frustumCulled; b.parent.add(m); m.bind(sk, b.bindMatrix); m.bindMatrix.copy(b.bindMatrix); m.bindMatrixInverse.copy(b.bindMatrixInverse); m.visible=false;
  R.userData.lfist={fist:m, open:hl}; return R.userData.lfist; };
P22.setLeftFist = function(R,on){ const F=R.userData.lfist; if(!F) return; F.fist.visible=!!on; F.open.visible=!on; };

P22.bowInfo = function(bow){ if(bow.userData.bi) return bow.userData.bi; const T=THREE; let m=null; bow.traverse(o=>{ if(o.isMesh&&!o.userData.p22) m=o; }); if(!m) return null;
  bow.updateMatrixWorld(true); const inv=new T.Matrix4().copy(bow.matrixWorld).invert(); const M=new T.Matrix4().multiplyMatrices(inv, m.matrixWorld); const pa=m.geometry.attributes.position, v=new T.Vector3(); let ymin=1e9,ymax=-1e9; const P=[];
  for(let i=0;i<pa.count;i++){ v.fromBufferAttribute(pa,i).applyMatrix4(M); P.push(v.clone()); ymin=Math.min(ymin,v.y); ymax=Math.max(ymax,v.y); }
  const H=ymax-ymin, top=new T.Vector3(), bot=new T.Vector3(), grip=new T.Vector3(); let nt=0,nb=0,ng=0; let gzmax=-1e9;
  for(const p of P){ if(p.y>ymax-0.03*H){ top.add(p); nt++; } if(p.y<ymin+0.03*H){ bot.add(p); nb++; } if(Math.abs(p.y-(ymin+ymax)/2)<0.05*H){ grip.add(p); ng++; gzmax=Math.max(gzmax,p.z);} }
  top.multiplyScalar(1/nt); bot.multiplyScalar(1/nb); grip.multiplyScalar(1/ng);
  // string sits on the side of the tips (archer side). shooting dir = from tips toward grip
  const sz=(top.z+bot.z)/2, dirSign=Math.sign(grip.z-sz)||1;
  const bi={top, bot, grip, H, dirSign, rest:new T.Vector3((top.x+bot.x)/2,(top.y+bot.y)/2,sz)}; bow.userData.bi=bi; return bi; };
// place bow in model-root space: grip centre at gp, bow Y along up, shooting dir along fwd
P22.bowPlace = function(S, bow, gp, up, fwd){ const T=THREE; const bi=P22.bowInfo(bow); if(!bi) return;
  const Y=up.clone().normalize(), Z=fwd.clone().multiplyScalar(bi.dirSign); Z.addScaledVector(Y,-Z.dot(Y)).normalize(); const X=new T.Vector3().crossVectors(Y,Z);
  const qr=new T.Quaternion().setFromRotationMatrix(new T.Matrix4().makeBasis(X,Y,Z)); // root-space orientation
  S.upd(); const qw=qr.clone().premultiply(S.rq); const pq=new T.Quaternion(); bow.parent.getWorldQuaternion(pq); bow.quaternion.copy(pq.invert().multiply(qw));
  bow.updateMatrixWorld(true); const gw=bi.grip.clone(); bow.localToWorld(gw); const tw=gp.clone(); S.root.localToWorld(tw); const d=tw.sub(gw); const bp=new T.Vector3(); bow.getWorldPosition(bp); bp.add(d); bow.parent.worldToLocal(bp); bow.position.copy(bp); bow.updateMatrixWorld(true); };
// string + arrow meshes (children of bow's parent space handled in world via root)
P22.bowFx = function(bow){ const T=THREE; if(bow.userData.fx) return bow.userData.fx;
  const sm=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xd9cfb0, roughness:0.8}); const cg=new T.CylinderGeometry(0.0035,0.0035,1,5,1,true); cg.translate(0,0.5,0);
  const s1=new T.Mesh(cg,sm), s2=new T.Mesh(cg,sm); s1.userData.p22=1; s2.userData.p22=1; s1.frustumCulled=s2.frustumCulled=false;
  const ar=new T.Group(); ar.userData.p22=1; const shaftM=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x8a6a42, roughness:0.7}), headM=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x8d8f94, metalness:0.7, roughness:0.35}), flM=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xe9e4d6, roughness:0.9, side:T.DoubleSide});
  const sh=new T.Mesh(new T.CylinderGeometry(0.0045,0.0045,0.74,6),shaftM); sh.rotation.x=Math.PI/2; sh.position.z=0.37; ar.add(sh);
  const hd=new T.Mesh(new T.ConeGeometry(0.011,0.05,6),headM); hd.rotation.x=Math.PI/2; hd.position.z=0.765; ar.add(hd);
  for(let k=0;k<3;k++){ const f=new T.Mesh(new T.PlaneGeometry(0.012,0.09),flM); f.position.set(0,0,0.065); f.rotation.set(Math.PI/2,0,0); const g=new T.Group(); g.rotation.z=k*2*Math.PI/3; f.position.y=0; f.translateX(0.008); g.add(f); ar.add(g); }
  ar.traverse(o=>{ o.userData.p22=1; o.frustumCulled=false; o.castShadow=true; });
  const fx={s1,s2,ar}; bow.userData.fx=fx; return fx; };
// update string (nock root-space point or null = rest) and arrow (visible flag)
P22.bowFxUpdate = function(S, bow, nock, arrowOn, vib){ const T=THREE; const bi=P22.bowInfo(bow), fx=P22.bowFx(bow); if(!bi) return;
  const par=bow.parent; for(const o of [fx.s1,fx.s2,fx.ar]) if(o.parent!==par) par.add(o);
  const tw=bi.top.clone(), bw=bi.bot.clone(); bow.localToWorld(tw); bow.localToWorld(bw);
  let nw; if(nock){ nw=nock.clone(); S.root.localToWorld(nw); } else { nw=bi.rest.clone(); if(vib){ nw.z+=vib*bi.dirSign*-1; } bow.localToWorld(nw); }
  const seg=(m,a,b)=>{ const pa=a.clone(), pb=b.clone(); par.worldToLocal(pa); par.worldToLocal(pb); const d=pb.clone().sub(pa), L=d.length(); m.position.copy(pa); m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new T.Vector3(0,1,0), d.normalize()); m.scale.set(1,L,1); m.updateMatrixWorld(true); };
  const ws=new T.Vector3(); par.getWorldScale(ws); const k=1/(ws.x||1); fx.s1.scale.x=fx.s1.scale.z=k; 
  seg(fx.s1,nw,tw); seg(fx.s2,nw,bw); fx.s1.scale.x=fx.s1.scale.z=fx.s2.scale.x=fx.s2.scale.z=k;
  fx.ar.visible=!!arrowOn; if(arrowOn){ const gw=bi.grip.clone(); bow.localToWorld(gw); const side=new T.Vector3(1,0,0).transformDirection(bow.matrixWorld).multiplyScalar(-0.018); // arrow rests beside the grip
     const ap=nw.clone(), at=gw.clone().add(side).add(new T.Vector3(0,1,0).transformDirection(bow.matrixWorld).multiplyScalar(0.03)); const d=at.sub(ap).normalize();
     const pa=ap.clone(); par.worldToLocal(pa); fx.ar.position.copy(pa); const pq=new T.Quaternion(); par.getWorldQuaternion(pq); const qw=new T.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new T.Vector3(0,0,1), d); fx.ar.quaternion.copy(pq.invert().multiply(qw)); fx.ar.scale.setScalar(k); fx.ar.updateMatrixWorld(true);
     fx.arDir=d; fx.arPos=ap; } };
P22.bowOff = function(bow){ const fx=bow&&bow.userData.fx; if(!fx) return; for(const o of [fx.s1,fx.s2,fx.ar]) if(o.parent) o.parent.remove(o); };

P22.FISTC_R=[2.84,4.35,-1.43]; P22.FISTC_L=[-2.84,4.35,-1.43];
P22.fistPt = function(S, sd){ const T=THREE; const h=S.b[sd==='L'?'LeftHand':'RightHand']; const c=sd==='L'?P22.FISTC_L:P22.FISTC_R; const v=new T.Vector3(c[0],c[1],c[2]); h.localToWorld(v); return S.root.worldToLocal(v); };
P22.fistAxis = function(S, sd){ const T=THREE; const a=sd==='L'?new T.Vector3(0.255,0.58,0.775).normalize():P22.SWAX.clone(); return S.axis(S.b[sd==='L'?'LeftHand':'RightHand'],a); };
P22.archer = function(S,p){ const T=THREE; const V=(x,y,z)=>new T.Vector3(x,y,z);
  P22.hipPos(S, p.hp[0],p.hp[1],p.hp[2]);
  P22.torso(S,{hp:p.lean*0.4, hy:p.hy, sp:[[p.lean*0.3,p.hy-4,0],[p.lean*0.3,p.hy-10,0],[0,p.hy-15,0]], nk:[4,p.hy*0.45,0], hd:[2+p.hp2,p.hdy,0]});
  P22.leg(S,'L',V(0.03,0.144,0.22),-55,34,V(0.1,0.6,1.2)); P22.leg(S,'R',V(-0.06,0.144,-0.24),70,34,V(-0.9,0.6,0.4));
  // bow arm: from shoulder toward target
  const sh=S.lp(S.b.LeftArm); const L=V(...p.bh).add(sh);
  const swl=V(0.255,0.58,0.775).normalize();
  P22.arm(S,'L',L,sh.clone().add(V(0.35,-0.45,0.1)), swl,V(...p.bup).normalize(), V(0,1,0),V(0.25,0,1));
  // draw hand
  const hd=S.lp(S.b.Head); const A=hd.clone().add(V(...p.dh)); const rs=S.lp(S.b.RightArm);
  P22.arm(S,'R',A,rs.clone().add(V(-0.25,0.15+p.elb,-0.6)), P22.SWAX,V(0,1,0), V(0,1,0),V(0.15,-0.1,1));
};
P22.BOWREADY={hp:[0.0,0.975,0.0], lean:4, hy:-72, hp2:0, hdy:-6, bh:[0.06,-0.12,0.50], bup:[-0.18,1,0.05], dh:[-0.05,-0.13,0.40], elb:-0.25};
P22.bowKeys = function(){ const B=P22.BOWREADY; const k=(t,o)=>Object.assign({t},B,o);
  const full={bh:[0.02,0.02,0.60], bup:[-0.12,1,0.0], dh:[-0.06,-0.085,0.07], elb:0.05, hdy:-2};
  return [k(0,{}), k(0.06,{bh:[0.03,0.0,0.58], bup:[-0.12,1,0.0], dh:[-0.03,-0.07,0.42]}), k(0.30,full), k(0.36,Object.assign({},full,{dh:[-0.10,-0.08,-0.01], elb:0.08})), k(0.50,Object.assign({},full,{dh:[-0.12,-0.09,-0.03], elb:0.08})), k(0.95,{})]; };
P22.BOW_REL=0.30;
P22.buildBow = function(S, R, clips){ const base=PA.sample(R, clips.Walk, 0.52); Object.assign(base, P22.capeAvg(R, clips.Walk, 16)); const cn=P22.capeBones(S).map(c=>c.o.name);
  const K=P22.bowKeys(), dur=K[K.length-1].t;
  return { BowShot: PA.bake(S,'BowShot',dur,30,P22.BODY.concat(cn),base,(S,t)=>{ P22.archer(S,P22.kf(K,t)); P22.capeIdleSway(S,t*2,0.04); P22.capePush(S); },{posNames:cn}) }; };

P22.horsePts = function(hg){ if(hg.userData.p22h) return hg.userData.p22h; const T=THREE; let tack=null, seat=null, rl=null, rr=null;
  let off=0; hg.traverse(o=>{ if(o.isSkinnedMesh&&(o.name==='Tack'||(o.parent&&o.parent.name==='Tack'))) tack=o; if(o.isSkinnedMesh&&o.userData.tackOff&&!tack){ tack=o; off=o.userData.tackOff; } if(o.name==='Seat') seat=o; if(o.name==='ReinL') rl=o; if(o.name==='ReinR') rr=o; });
  const H={tack,seat,rl,rr,st:[-1,-1]}; if(tack){ const pa=tack.geometry.attributes.position, v=new T.Vector3(); hg.updateMatrixWorld(true); const inv=new T.Matrix4().copy(hg.matrixWorld).invert(); let best=[[1e9,-1],[1e9,-1]];
    for(let i=off;i<pa.count;i++){ tack.getVertexPosition(i,v); tack.localToWorld(v); v.applyMatrix4(inv); if(Math.abs(v.z-(seat?0:0))>0.6) continue; const sd=v.x>0?0:1; if(Math.abs(v.x)<0.12) continue; if(v.y<best[sd][0]) best[sd]=[v.y,i]; }
    H.st=[best[0][1],best[1][1]]; H.stY=[best[0][0],best[1][0]]; }
  hg.userData.p22h=H; return H; };
P22.stirrup = function(hg, sd){ const T=THREE; const H=P22.horsePts(hg); const i=H.st[sd==='L'?0:1]; if(!H.tack||i<0) return null; const v=new T.Vector3(); H.tack.getVertexPosition(i,v); return H.tack.localToWorld(v); };
// seated rider pose. k = gallop 0..1, t = time, ph = mount phase 0..1 (1 = seated)
P22.ridePose = function(S, hg, k, t, o){ const T=THREE; const V=(x,y,z)=>new T.Vector3(x,y,z); o=o||{};
  const H=P22.horsePts(hg); S.upd(); const toR=w=>S.root.worldToLocal(w.clone());
  const sw=new T.Vector3(); H.seat.getWorldPosition(sw); const seat=toR(sw);
  const bob=k*0.025*Math.sin(t*2*Math.PI*1.9);
  P22.hipPos(S, seat.x, seat.y+0.075+bob, seat.z-0.02);
  const lean=6+k*16; P22.torso(S,{hp:lean*0.5, sp:[[lean*0.3,0,0],[lean*0.3,0,0],[lean*0.2,0,0]], nk:[2,0,0], hd:[-(lean*0.6)+2,0,0]});
  for(const sd of ['L','R']){ const sg=sd==='L'?1:-1; let st=P22.stirrup(hg,sd); let ank;
    if(st){ ank=toR(st).add(V(0,0.07,-0.02)); } else ank=seat.clone().add(V(sg*0.3,-0.62,0.02));
    const hp=S.lp(S.b[(sd==='L'?'Left':'Right')+'UpLeg']); P22.leg(S,sd,ank,10,22,hp.clone().add(V(sg*0.9,-0.2,1.2)));
  }
  // reins
  for(const sd of ['L','R']){ const sg=sd==='L'?1:-1; const rn=sd==='L'?H.rl:H.rr; const w=new T.Vector3(); rn.getWorldPosition(w); const g=toR(w).add(V(0,0.02,-0.03));
    // hand pivot so that fist centre lands on rein grip: iterate twice
    let tgt=g.clone(); const L=sd==='L'?'Left':'Right';
    for(let it=0;it<3;it++){ P22.arm(S,sd,tgt,S.lp(S.b[L+'Arm']).add(V(sg*0.35,-0.5,-0.3)), sd==='L'?V(0.255,0.58,0.775).normalize():P22.SWAX, V(-sg*0.12,1,0.35).normalize(), V(0,1,0), V(-sg*0.4,-0.3,1)); const f=P22.fistPt(S,sd); tgt.add(g.clone().sub(f)); }
  }
};
// cape flow behind (root space): rotate rows back around shoulder line, flutter with speed
P22.capeFlow = function(S, k, t, floorY){ const T=THREE; const C=P22.capeBones(S); const piv=new T.Vector3(); let n=0; for(const c of C) if(c.row===0){ piv.add(S.lp(c.o)); n++; } piv.multiplyScalar(1/n);
  const base=0.45+0.45*k; // radians at hem
  P22.capeSwing(S,new T.Vector3(1,0,0),piv,(row,col)=>{ const u=row/8; return (base*Math.pow(u,1.1) + (0.05+0.12*k)*u*Math.sin(t*(6+8*k)-row*0.7+col*0.25)); });
  if(floorY!==undefined){ for(const c of C){ const p=S.lp(c.o); if(p.y<floorY){ p.y=floorY; S.root.localToWorld(p); c.o.parent.worldToLocal(p); c.o.position.copy(p); c.o.updateMatrixWorld(true);} } } };

P22.MOUNT = [ // v22b: صعود نظيف — القدم اليسرى بالركاب، الرجل اليمنى تمرّ فوق الكفل، الجسم منتصب والوجه للأمام
 {t:0.00, h:[0.62,-0.56,-0.04], yaw:-48, lean:3, la:[0.42,-1.36,0.02], lsw:0, ra:[0.62,-1.36,-0.14], rsw:0, lh:[0.30,-0.20,0.12], lrw:0, rh:[0.42,-0.22,-0.12], rrw:0, hd:0},
 {t:0.22, h:[0.60,-0.52,-0.05], yaw:-50, lean:6, la:[0.33,-0.52,-0.03], lsw:1, ra:[0.64,-1.34,-0.14], rsw:0, lh:[0.16,0.10,0.18], lrw:0, rh:[0.10,0.10,-0.30], rrw:0, hd:0},
 {t:0.48, h:[0.40,0.18,-0.06], yaw:-36, lean:8, la:[0.33,-0.52,-0.03], lsw:1, ra:[0.36,-0.40,-0.40], rsw:0, lh:[0.14,0.10,0.18], lrw:0, rh:[0.04,0.10,-0.30], rrw:0, hd:0},

 {t:1.10, h:[0,0.075,-0.02], yaw:0, lean:6, la:[0.33,-0.52,-0.03], lsw:1, ra:[-0.33,-0.52,-0.03], rsw:1, lh:[0.06,0.12,0.36], lrw:1, rh:[-0.06,0.12,0.36], rrw:1, hd:0} ];
P22.MOUNT_DUR = 1.10;

P22.horseHM = function(hg){ if(hg.userData.p22hm) return hg.userData.p22hm; const T=THREE; const H=P22.horsePts(hg); hg.updateMatrixWorld(true);   // v23: خريطة ارتفاع ظهر الحصان + عرض جسمه عند السرج (للعباءة والساقين)
  const inv=new T.Matrix4().copy(hg.matrixWorld).invert(), sw=new T.Vector3(); H.seat.getWorldPosition(sw); const sl=sw.clone().applyMatrix4(inv);
  const d=0.04, x0=sl.x-0.6, z0=sl.z+0.5, nx=31, nz=60, top=new Float32Array(nx*nz).fill(-1e9), y0=sl.y-1.1, ny=34, wy=new Float32Array(ny).fill(0), v=new T.Vector3();
  hg.traverse(o=>{ if(!o.isMesh||!o.visible) return; const pa=o.geometry.attributes.position; for(let i=0;i<pa.count;i++){ if(o.isSkinnedMesh) o.getVertexPosition(i,v); else v.fromBufferAttribute(pa,i); o.localToWorld(v); v.applyMatrix4(inv);
    const ix=Math.round((v.x-x0)/d), iz=Math.round((z0-v.z)/d); if(ix>=0&&ix<nx&&iz>=0&&iz<nz){ const j=iz*nx+ix; if(v.y>top[j]) top[j]=v.y; }
    if(Math.abs(v.z-sl.z)<0.14){ const iy=Math.round((v.y-y0)/d); if(iy>=0&&iy<ny){ const ax=Math.abs(v.x-sl.x); if(ax>wy[iy]) wy[iy]=ax; } } } });
  for(let pass=0;pass<2;pass++){ const cp=top.slice(), g=(z,x)=>(z<0||z>=nz||x<0||x>=nx)?-1e9:cp[z*nx+x]; for(let iz=0;iz<nz;iz++) for(let ix=0;ix<nx;ix++){ const j=iz*nx+ix; if(cp[j]>-1e8) continue; const l=g(iz,ix-1), r=g(iz,ix+1), f=g(iz-1,ix), b=g(iz+1,ix); if(l>-1e8&&r>-1e8) top[j]=Math.max(l,r); else if(f>-1e8&&b>-1e8) top[j]=Math.max(f,b); } }   // ثقوب داخلية فقط، بدون تضخيم الحواف
  for(let iy=ny-2;iy>=0;iy--) if(wy[iy]<0.05) wy[iy]=wy[iy+1];
  let sk=null; hg.traverse(o=>{ if(o.isSkinnedMesh&&!sk) sk=o.skeleton; }); const bb=[]; if(sk){ for(const nm of ['spine1','pelvis']){ const b=sk.bones.find(x=>x.name===nm); if(b){ b.updateMatrixWorld(true); bb.push({b, rest:new T.Matrix4().copy(inv).multiply(b.matrixWorld), z:b.getWorldPosition(new T.Vector3()).applyMatrix4(inv).z}); } } }
  const M={sl,d,x0,z0,nx,nz,top,y0,ny,wy,inv,bb};
  M.h=(x,z)=>{ const fx=(x-x0)/d, fz=(z0-z)/d, ix=Math.floor(fx), iz=Math.floor(fz); if(ix<0||iz<0||ix>=nx-1||iz>=nz-1) return -1e9; const ax=fx-ix, az=fz-iz;
    const g=(a,b)=>top[(iz+a)*nx+ix+b]; const c=[g(0,0),g(0,1),g(1,0),g(1,1)]; if(c.some(q=>q<-1e8)) return Math.max(...c); return (c[0]*(1-ax)+c[1]*ax)*(1-az)+(c[2]*(1-ax)+c[3]*ax)*az; };
  M.w=(y)=>{ const iy=Math.max(0,Math.min(ny-1,Math.round((y-y0)/d))); let m=wy[iy]; if(iy>0) m=Math.max(m,wy[iy-1]); if(iy<ny-1) m=Math.max(m,wy[iy+1]); return m; };
  hg.userData.p22hm=M; return M; };
// stirrup point OUTSIDE the horse cover (root space): foot hangs along the horse's side, visible
P22.stirPt = function(S, hg, sd){ const T=THREE; const M=P22.horseHM(hg); const sg=sd==='L'?1:-1; const p=M.sl.clone(); p.y-=0.50; p.x+=sg*(M.w(p.y)+0.075); p.z+=0.0;
  p.applyMatrix4(hg.matrixWorld); return S.root.worldToLocal(p); };
// signed clearance of a root-space point above the horse's back (metres); +∞ when not over the horse
P22.horseClear = function(S, hg, clr){ const T=THREE; const M=P22.horseHM(hg); hg.updateMatrixWorld(true); S.root.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const hinv=new T.Matrix4().copy(hg.matrixWorld).invert(), m=hinv.clone().multiply(S.root.matrixWorld), q=new T.Vector3(), q1=new T.Vector3(), q2=new T.Vector3();
  const D=M.bb.map(o=>{ o.b.updateMatrixWorld(true); const cur=hinv.clone().multiply(o.b.matrixWorld); return o.rest.clone().multiply(cur.invert()); });   // يتبع ظهر الحصان المتحرك (العمود الفقري والحوض) أثناء العدو
  return (p)=>{ q.copy(p).applyMatrix4(m); if(D.length===2){ q1.copy(q).applyMatrix4(D[0]); q2.copy(q).applyMatrix4(D[1]); const w=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(M.bb[0].z-q.z)/(M.bb[0].z-M.bb[1].z))); q.copy(q1).lerp(q2,w); }
    const h=M.h(q.x,q.z); return h<-1e8 ? 1e9 : q.y-h-clr; }; };
// cape on the horse: every column drapes by itself onto the back/rump surface, widening over the croup like real cloth; streams with speed
P22.capeRide = function(S, k, t, Fd, wt){ const T=THREE; const C=P22.capeBones(S); if(wt===undefined) wt=1;
  const grid=[]; let maxR=0; for(const c of C){ (grid[c.row]=grid[c.row]||[])[c.col]={c, p:S.lp(c.o), q:S.lq(c.o)}; maxR=Math.max(maxR,c.row); }
  let cx=0, n=0; for(const g of grid[0]) if(g){ cx+=g.p.x; n++; } cx/=n;
  const base=(0.30+0.75*k)*wt, fl=(0.03+0.14*k)*wt, X=new T.Vector3(1,0,0);
  const rot=(v,a)=>{ const cs=Math.cos(a), sn=Math.sin(a); return new T.Vector3(v.x, v.y*cs-v.z*sn, v.y*sn+v.z*cs); };
  const P=[grid[0].map(g=>g&&g.p.clone())], A=[grid[0].map(()=>0)];
  for(let r=1;r<=maxR;r++){ const row=grid[r]; if(!row){ P[r]=P[r-1]; A[r]=A[r-1]; continue; } const u=r/maxR, spr=1+0.55*wt*Math.max(0,u-0.3)/0.7;
    P[r]=[]; A[r]=[];
    row.forEach((g,ci)=>{ if(!g||!P[r-1][ci]||!grid[r-1][ci]){ P[r][ci]=null; return; } const seg=g.p.clone().sub(grid[r-1][ci].p); const prevSpr=1+0.55*wt*Math.max(0,(r-1)/maxR-0.3)/0.7;
      seg.x += (g.p.x-cx)*(spr-1) - (grid[r-1][ci].p.x-cx)*(prevSpr-1); seg.multiplyScalar(1-0.22*wt*Math.max(0,u-0.4)/0.6);   // الطرف لا يصل لجذر الذيل
      const fa=fl*u*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(t*(6+8*k)-r*0.7+ci*0.25)); const pl=(a)=>P[r-1][ci].clone().add(rot(seg,a));
      let a=base*Math.pow(u,1.1)+fa, p=pl(a);
      if(Fd&&Fd(p)<0){ let lo=a, hi=2.0; for(let i=0;i<14;i++){ const m=(lo+hi)/2; if(Fd(pl(m))<0) lo=m; else hi=m; } a=hi; p=pl(a); }
      P[r][ci]=p; A[r][ci]=a; }); }
  for(let r=1;r<=maxR;r++){ const row=grid[r]; if(!row) continue; row.forEach((g,ci)=>{ if(!g||!P[r][ci]) return; const np=P[r][ci].clone(); S.root.localToWorld(np); g.c.o.parent.worldToLocal(np); g.c.o.position.copy(np); S.setLq(g.c.o, g.q.clone().premultiply(new T.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(X, A[r][ci]))); }); } };

P22.ridePose = function(S, hg, k, t, mt){ const T=THREE; const V=(x,y,z)=>new T.Vector3(x,y,z);
  const H=P22.horsePts(hg); S.upd(); const toR=w=>S.root.worldToLocal(w.clone());
  const sw=new T.Vector3(); H.seat.getWorldPosition(sw); const seat=toR(sw);
  const p = P22.kf(P22.MOUNT, mt===undefined?9:mt); const seated = mt===undefined || mt>=P22.MOUNT_DUR;
  const bob = seated? k*0.025*Math.sin(t*2*Math.PI*1.9) : 0;
  P22.hipPos(S, seat.x+p.h[0], seat.y+p.h[1]+bob, seat.z+p.h[2]);
  const lean = seated ? 6+k*16 : p.lean;
  P22.torso(S,{hp:lean*0.5, hy:p.yaw, sp:[[lean*0.3,p.yaw*0.8,0],[lean*0.3,p.yaw*0.6,0],[lean*0.2,p.yaw*0.4,0]], nk:[2,-p.yaw*0.3,0], hd:[-(lean*0.6)+2,-p.yaw*0.35,0]});
  for(const sd of ['L','R']){ const sg=sd==='L'?1:-1; const st=P22.stirrup(hg,sd); const a=sd==='L'?p.la:p.ra, w=sd==='L'?p.lsw:p.rsw;
    let ank=seat.clone().add(V(...a)); if(w>0) ank.lerp(P22.stirPt(S,hg,sd), w);
    const hp=S.lp(S.b[(sd==='L'?'Left':'Right')+'UpLeg']); const out = seated ? sg*0.9 : sg*0.6; P22.leg(S,sd,ank,10,seated?22:30,hp.clone().add(V(out,-0.2,1.2))); }
  for(const sd of ['L','R']){ const sg=sd==='L'?1:-1; const rn=sd==='L'?H.rl:H.rr; const w=new T.Vector3(); rn.getWorldPosition(w); const rein=toR(w).add(V(0,0.02,-0.03));
    const rel=sd==='L'?p.lh:p.rh, rw=sd==='L'?p.lrw:p.rrw; const g=seat.clone().add(V(...rel)).lerp(rein, rw);
    let tgt=g.clone(); const L=sd==='L'?'Left':'Right';
    for(let it=0;it<3;it++){ P22.arm(S,sd,tgt,S.lp(S.b[L+'Arm']).add(V(sg*0.35,-0.5,-0.3)), sd==='L'?V(0.255,0.58,0.775).normalize():P22.SWAX, V(-sg*0.12,1,0.35).normalize(), V(0,1,0), V(-sg*0.4,-0.3,1)); const f=P22.fistPt(S,sd); tgt.add(g.clone().sub(f)); } }
};

// ═══ v24 (R24): صعود/نزول أبطأ وأوضح، قدما الفارس في ركابَي الحصان الجديدين، ميل للأمام بالعدو، عباءة على الكفل لا تغطي الساقين ═══
P22.TREAD = [0.367, 0.878, 0.183];   // ركاب خيول الجولة 5 (إحداثيات الحصان المحلية)
P22.treadW = function(hg, sd){ const T=THREE; const H=P22.horsePts(hg); if(!H.tack) return null; const k='p24t'+sd; let U=hg.userData[k]; const sg=sd==='L'?1:-1; const tk=H.tack;
  hg.updateMatrixWorld(true); const inv=new T.Matrix4().copy(hg.matrixWorld).invert(), v=new T.Vector3();
  if(!U){ const pa=tk.geometry.attributes.position, off=tk.userData.tackOff||0, tg=new T.Vector3(sg*P22.TREAD[0], P22.TREAD[1], P22.TREAD[2]); let bi=-1, bd=1e9;
    for(let i=off;i<pa.count;i++){ tk.getVertexPosition(i,v); tk.localToWorld(v); v.applyMatrix4(inv); const d=v.distanceToSquared(tg); if(d<bd){ bd=d; bi=i; } }
    if(bi<0) return null; tk.getVertexPosition(bi,v); tk.localToWorld(v); v.applyMatrix4(inv); U=hg.userData[k]={i:bi, d:tg.clone().sub(v), dist:Math.sqrt(bd)}; }
  tk.getVertexPosition(U.i,v); tk.localToWorld(v); v.applyMatrix4(inv).add(U.d); return v.applyMatrix4(hg.matrixWorld); };
// ankle target (root space) so the ball of the foot stands on the tread
P22.stirPt = function(S, hg, sd){ const T=THREE; const w=P22.treadW(hg, sd); if(!w){ const M=P22.horseHM(hg); const sg=sd==='L'?1:-1; const p=M.sl.clone(); p.y-=0.50; p.x+=sg*(M.w(p.y)+0.075); p.applyMatrix4(hg.matrixWorld); return S.root.worldToLocal(p); }
  const r=S.root.worldToLocal(w); r.y+=0.075; r.z-=0.07; const sg=sd==='L'?1:-1; r.x-=sg*0.01; return r; };
P22.MOUNT_DUR = 2.30;
P22.mountKeys = function(SH){ const g=-SH, hp=0.95-SH, an=g+0.09;   // نسبة إلى السرج؛ g = الأرض
  return [
 {t:0.00, h:[0.62,hp,0.10], yaw:-62, lean:3, la:[0.46,an,0.20], lsw:0, ra:[0.70,an,-0.02], rsw:0, lh:[0.52,hp+0.30,0.30], lrw:0, rh:[0.48,hp+0.28,0.02], rrw:0},

 {t:1.30, h:[0.36,0.26,0.02], yaw:-46, lean:20, la:[0.46,an,0.20], lsw:1, ra:[0.42,-0.58,-0.14], rsw:0, lh:[0.10,0.12,0.40], lrw:0, rh:[0.10,0.06,-0.22], rrw:0},
